//! Creation is a write even when an edit operation has the stronger guard.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#151-every-executed-write — conditional advice never infers creation policy.
use super::*;
pub(super) fn notes(sources:&[(String,String)],bound:&Bound)->Vec<Diagnostic>{
    let documents=sources.iter().map(|(file,text)|crate::parse_document(file,text).0).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let catalog=Catalog::build(documents.clone(),&mut Vec::new());
    let mut writes=Vec::new();
    for doc in &documents{for p in &doc.programs{if p.kind!=ast::ProgramKind::Mutation{continue;}
        for (write,context) in super::authority_walk::writes(&catalog,p){
            let ast::Expr::Write{op,table,fields,site,..}=write else{continue;};
            let Some(info)=catalog.tables.get(&table)else{continue;};
            let requirements=super::operation::requirements(&catalog,info,&context);
            let t=&bound.schema.tables[&table];
            let gate=if op==ast::WriteOp::Update{t.update_rule_judged().into_owned()}else{t.rules.insert.clone()};
            let admission=RuleExpr::And(requirements.into_iter().chain([gate]).collect());
            writes.push((p.name.clone(),op,table,fields,site,admission));
        }
    }}
    fn necessary(rule:&RuleExpr)->RuleExpr{match rule{
        RuleExpr::And(xs)=>RuleExpr::And(xs.iter().map(necessary).collect()),
        _ if super::operation::safe(rule)=>rule.clone(),_=>RuleExpr::Allow,
    }}
    // Recover only column-specific conditional guards; ordinary table-wide
    // differences keep their existing inventory code and priority.
    let mut specific=BTreeMap::<(String,String,String),(bool,Vec<RuleExpr>)>::new();
    for doc in &documents{for p in &doc.programs{if p.kind!=ast::ProgramKind::Mutation{continue;}
        let plain=super::authority_walk::writes(&catalog,p);
        let columns=plain.iter().filter_map(|(e,_)|if let ast::Expr::Write{op:ast::WriteOp::Update,fields,..}=e{Some(fields.iter().map(|f|f.name.clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>())}else{None}).flatten().collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
        for column in columns{for ((write,context),(_,base)) in super::authority_walk::column_writes(&catalog,p,&column).into_iter().zip(&plain){
            let ast::Expr::Write{op:ast::WriteOp::Update,table,fields,..}=write else{continue;};
            if !fields.iter().any(|f|f.name==column){continue;}
            let requirements=super::operation::requirements(&catalog,&catalog.tables[&table],&context);
            let restriction=RuleExpr::And(requirements.into_iter().chain([bound.schema.tables[&table].update_rule_judged().into_owned()]).collect());
            let entry=specific.entry((p.name.clone(),table,column.clone())).or_default();entry.0|=context.body.len()>base.body.len();entry.1.push(necessary(&restriction));
        }}
    }}
    let mut out=Vec::new();let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();let mut replaced=BTreeSet::new();
    for (name,op,table,fields,site,admission) in &writes{
        if !matches!(op,ast::WriteOp::Insert|ast::WriteOp::Upsert){continue;}
        for ((editor,target,column),(conditional,guards)) in &specific{
            if !conditional{continue;}
            let guard=RuleExpr::Or(guards.clone());
            if target!=table||!fields.iter().any(|f|&f.name==column&&supplied(f,name,&documents)){continue;}
            let guard=necessary(&guard);
            if kernel::implies(admission,&guard)!=Ok(false)||!seen.insert((name.clone(),table.clone(),BTreeSet::from([column.clone()]),guard.quarry())){continue;}
            let audience=bound.schema.tables[table].column_reads.get(column);
            let private=audience.is_some_and(|r|kernel::implies(admission,r)==Ok(false));
            let code=if private{"N_PRIVATE_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY"}else{"N_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY"};
            let detail=if private{format!("; write-only private payload: creator admission does not imply column read audience {}",audience.unwrap().quarry())}else{String::new()};
            out.push(Diagnostic::new(site,code,format!("{name}: argument-supplied {table}.{column} is open at create/upsert, but {editor} requires {} when this column changes{detail}",guard.quarry()),"confirm this column's creation authority against the specification; apply its change guard at creation too when required, declare independent column write intent, then run test with a non-null value").guide("security"));
            replaced.insert((name.clone(),table.clone(),column.clone()));
        }
    }
    seen.clear();
    for (name,op,table,fields,site,admission) in &writes{
        if !matches!(op,ast::WriteOp::Insert|ast::WriteOp::Upsert){continue;}
        for (editor,edit,other,patch,_,restricted) in &writes{
            if *edit!=ast::WriteOp::Update||other!=table{continue;}
            let restriction=necessary(restricted);
            if kernel::implies(&RuleExpr::Allow,&restriction)==Ok(true)||kernel::implies(admission,&restriction)!=Ok(false){continue;}
            let columns=patch.iter().filter(|f|!replaced.contains(&(name.clone(),table.clone(),f.name.clone()))&&fields.iter().any(|insert|insert.name==f.name&&supplied(insert,name,&documents))).map(|f|f.name.clone()).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
            if columns.is_empty()||!seen.insert((name.clone(),table.clone(),columns.clone(),restriction.quarry())){continue;}
            out.push(Diagnostic::new(site,"N_INSERT_WRITE_INTENT",format!("{name}: {table}.{} can be supplied at insert/upsert without the edit restriction of {editor}: {}; creation admission: {}",columns.into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(","),restriction.quarry(),admission.quarry()),"confirm whether the spec permits different creation authority; otherwise guard every insert/upsert arm too and declare column write only intent, then run snapback4 test").guide("security"));
        }
    }out
}

fn supplied(field:&ast::ObjectField,program:&str,documents:&[ast::Document])->bool{
    let Some(value)=&field.value else{return true;};let mut value=value.clone();let mut supplied=false;
    super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut value,&mut |e|if let ast::Expr::Name{name,..}=e{supplied|=documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.programs).filter(|p|p.name==program).any(|p|p.args.iter().any(|a|a.name==*name));});
    supplied
}
//! Individually justified source-bound dispositions; never proof or authority.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#162-narrow-gates-and-individual-dispositions — bulk compiler-note review cannot clear security gates.
use super::*;
use serde::{Serialize,Deserialize};
use sha2::{Digest,Sha256};
const ANALYZER:&str="sec7-4";
#[derive(Serialize,Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
struct File{version:u32,entries:BTreeMap<String,Entry>}
#[derive(Serialize,Deserialize)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
struct Entry{sources:String,analyzer:String,reason:String}
#[derive(Serialize)]
pub(super) struct Disposition{pub id:String,pub code:String,pub site:String,pub gated:bool,pub acknowledged:bool,pub reason:Option<String>}
pub(super) fn gated(code:&str)->bool{matches!(code,"N_RETAINED_ROLE_GRANT"|"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE")}
fn hash(bytes:&[u8])->String{format!("{:x}",Sha256::digest(bytes))}
fn digest(s:&str)->bool{s.len()==64&&s.bytes().all(|b|b.is_ascii_hexdigit())}
fn id(n:&snapback4_lang::Diagnostic)->String{hash(&serde_json::to_vec(&serde_json::to_value(n).unwrap()).unwrap())}
pub(super) fn review(project:&Project,sources:&[(String,String)],notes:&[snapback4_lang::Diagnostic],ack:Option<(&str,&str)>)->Result<Vec<Disposition>,String>{
    if project.sources().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?!=sources{return Err("Quarry sources changed during security review; rerun check".into());}
    let mut sorted=sources.to_vec();sorted.sort_by(|a,b|a.0.cmp(&b.0));
    let fingerprint=hash(&serde_json::to_vec(&sorted).unwrap());
    let path=project.schema_dir().join("security-reviews.json");
    // Keep the lock inode in ignored generated output. Do not unlink a lock
    // while another process can be waiting on that inode. Kernel ownership
    // releases it on exit, including an interrupted writer.
    let _writer_lock=if ack.is_some(){
        let directory=project.schema_dir().join("generated");
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&directory).map_err(|e|format!("cannot create security review lock directory: {e}"))?;
        let lock=std::fs::OpenOptions::new().create(true).truncate(false).read(true).write(true).open(directory.join("security-reviews.lock")).map_err(|e|format!("cannot open security review lock: {e}"))?;
        lock.lock().map_err(|e|format!("cannot acquire security review lock: {e}"))?;Some(lock)
    }else{None};
    if project.sources().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?!=sources{return Err("Quarry sources changed while waiting for security review; rerun check".into());}
    let mut file:File=match std::fs::read(&path){
        Ok(bytes)=>serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).map_err(|_|"invalid snapback/security-reviews.json; expected version 1 with per-note source/analyzer/reason records".to_string())?,
        Err(e) if e.kind()==std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound=>File{version:1,entries:BTreeMap::new()},
        Err(e)=>return Err(format!("cannot read security dispositions: {e}")),
    };
    if file.version!=1||file.entries.iter().any(|(key,e)|!digest(key)||!digest(&e.sources)||e.reason.trim().is_empty()||e.analyzer.is_empty()){return Err("invalid security disposition version/digest/reason".into());}
    if let Some((key,reason))=ack{
        if reason.trim().is_empty(){return Err("--reason needs the specification rationale for this individual note".into());}
        if !notes.iter().any(|n|id(n)==key){return Err("--ack-security ID is not a current security note; run check --json".into());}
        file.entries.insert(key.into(),Entry{sources:fingerprint.clone(),analyzer:ANALYZER.into(),reason:reason.trim().into()});
        let temporary=path.with_extension(format!("json.{}.tmp",std::process::id()));
        let result=std::fs::write(&temporary,serde_json::to_vec_pretty(&file).unwrap()).and_then(|_|{
            if project.sources()?!=sources{return Err(std::io::Error::other("Quarry sources changed before disposition publication; rerun check"));}
            std::fs::rename(&temporary,&path)
        });
        if let Err(e)=result{let _=std::fs::remove_file(&temporary);return Err(format!("cannot record security disposition: {e}"));}
    }
    Ok(notes.iter().map(|n|{let key=id(n);let entry=file.entries.get(&key).filter(|e|e.sources==fingerprint&&e.analyzer==ANALYZER);
        Disposition{id:key,code:n.code.clone(),site:n.site.to_string(),gated:gated(&n.code),acknowledged:entry.is_some(),reason:entry.map(|e|e.reason.clone())}
    }).collect())
}
pub(super) fn pending(dispositions:&[Disposition])->bool{dispositions.iter().any(|d|d.gated&&!d.acknowledged)}
pub(super) fn print(dispositions:&[Disposition]){for d in dispositions.iter().filter(|d|d.gated||d.acknowledged){
    if d.acknowledged{println!("security reviewed {} {}: {}",d.id,d.code,d.reason.as_deref().unwrap_or(""));}
    else{println!("security review required {} {} at {}; repair or check --ack-security {} --reason '<spec rationale>'",d.id,d.code,d.site,d.id);}
}}
#[cfg(test)]mod tests{
use super::*;
struct Scratch(std::path::PathBuf);impl Drop for Scratch{fn drop(&mut self){let _=std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);}}
#[test]fn individual_source_and_diagnostic_bound_dispositions(){
 let root=std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-sec7-dispositions-{}",std::process::id()));std::fs::create_dir_all(root.join("snapback")).unwrap();
 let _scratch=Scratch(root.clone());let p=Project{root:root.clone()};std::fs::write(p.schema_dir().join("schema.q"),"query q():\n  return true\n").unwrap();let sources=p.sources().unwrap();
 let a=snapback4_lang::Diagnostic::new(&Default::default(),"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE","a","");let b=snapback4_lang::Diagnostic::new(&Default::default(),"N_RETAINED_ROLE_GRANT","b","");let notes=[a.clone(),b];
 assert!(review(&p,&sources,&notes,Some((&id(&a),"  "))).is_err());assert!(review(&p,&sources,&notes,Some(("unknown","intended"))).is_err());
 let ds=review(&p,&sources,&notes,Some((&id(&a),"the specification allows optional draft evidence"))).unwrap();assert!(ds[0].acknowledged);assert!(!ds[1].acknowledged);assert!(pending(&ds));
 let changed=[snapback4_lang::Diagnostic::new(&Default::default(),"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE","changed","")];assert!(!review(&p,&sources,&changed,None).unwrap()[0].acknowledged);
 let path=p.schema_dir().join("security-reviews.json");let mut f:File=serde_json::from_slice(&std::fs::read(&path).unwrap()).unwrap();for old in ["sec7-2","sec7-3"]{f.entries.get_mut(&id(&a)).unwrap().analyzer=old.into();std::fs::write(&path,serde_json::to_vec(&f).unwrap()).unwrap();assert!(!review(&p,&sources,&notes,None).unwrap()[0].acknowledged);}
 std::fs::write(p.schema_dir().join("schema.q"),"query q():\n  return false\n").unwrap();assert!(review(&p,&sources,&notes,Some((&id(&a),"intended"))).is_err());assert!(!review(&p,&p.sources().unwrap(),&notes,None).unwrap()[0].acknowledged);
 std::fs::write(&path,"{}").unwrap();assert!(review(&p,&p.sources().unwrap(),&notes,None).is_err());std::fs::remove_dir_all(root).unwrap();
}
}
//! Wave-23 day-two regressions; source copies contain schema only.
use snapback4_lang::{compile,security::{self,challenges::{self,Campaign}}};
fn bound(s:&str)->snapback4_lang::Bound{let b=compile(&[("schema.q".into(),s.into())]);assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",b.diagnostics);b}
fn notes(s:&str)->security::Report{let b=bound(s);let mut r=security::analyze_rules(&b);security::add_source_notes(&mut r,&[("schema.q".into(),s.into())],&b);r}
fn has(s:&str,code:&str)->bool{notes(s).notes.iter().any(|n|n.code==code)}
const LOOSEN:&[&str]=&[
 include_str!("security/wave23/003.q"),include_str!("security/wave23/007.q"),include_str!("security/wave23/011.q"),include_str!("security/wave23/015.q"),
 include_str!("security/wave23/019.q"),include_str!("security/wave23/023.q"),include_str!("security/wave23/027.q"),include_str!("security/wave23/031.q"),
 include_str!("security/wave23/035.q"),include_str!("security/wave23/039.q"),include_str!("security/wave23/043.q"),include_str!("security/wave23/047.q"),
];
#[test]fn all_twelve_loosen_lanes_have_no_optional_upload_gate_with_own_or_no_intent(){
 for (i,s) in LOOSEN.iter().enumerate(){for s in [s.to_string(),s.lines().filter(|l|!l.trim_start().starts_with("invariant ")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("\n")]{assert!(!has(&s,"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE"),"loosen lane index {i}");}}
}
const OPTIONAL:&str="table docs:\n  photo: image <=10000 ?\n  owner: principal\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\nmutation add(photo: image <=10000 ?):\n  return insert docs {photo,owner:viewer}\n";
#[test]fn optional_media_is_not_required_by_a_name_but_exact_nonnull_intent_is(){
 assert!(!has(OPTIONAL,"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE"));
 for predicate in [".photo != null","not (.photo = null)",".photo != null and .owner = viewer"]{assert!(has(&format!("{OPTIONAL}invariant docs always {predicate}\n"),"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE"),"{predicate}");}
 for predicate in [".photo != null or .owner = viewer","not (.photo != null)",".owner != null"]{assert!(!has(&format!("{OPTIONAL}invariant docs always {predicate}\n"),"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE"),"{predicate}");}
 assert!(!has(&OPTIONAL.replace("photo: image <=10000 ?):","photo: image <=10000):"),"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE"));
}
const CHILD:&str="table parents:\n  n: int default 0\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\ntable photos:\n  parent: parents\n  photo: image <=10000\n  unique parent\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  delete <- allow\n  online only\nmutation add(photo: image <=10000 ?):\n  p = insert parents {}\n  if photo != null:\n    insert photos {parent:p.id,photo}\n  return p\n";
#[test]fn required_child_columns_do_not_make_an_optional_attachment_mandatory(){
 assert!(!has(CHILD,"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE"));assert!(has(CHILD,"N_OPTIONAL_EVIDENCE"));
 let required=format!("{CHILD}invariant parents always exists photos[parent = .id]\n");assert!(has(&required,"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE"));
 let guarded=required.replace("  p = insert parents {}","  require photo != null else REQUIRED\n  p = insert parents {}");assert!(!has(&guarded,"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE"));
}
#[test]fn mandatory_child_discriminators_do_not_gate_unrelated_optional_attachments(){
 let s=CHILD.replace("  parent: parents", "  kind: enum('checkout','return')\n  parent: parents").replace("  unique parent", "  unique parent, kind").replace("photos {parent:p.id,photo}","photos {parent:p.id,kind:'return',photo}");
 let s=format!("{s}invariant parents always exists photos[parent = .id, kind = 'checkout']\n");assert!(!has(&s,"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE"));
 let checkout=s.replace("kind:'return'","kind:'checkout'");assert!(has(&checkout,"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE"));
 let sibling=checkout.replace("add(photo: image <=10000 ?)","add(photo: image <=10000 ?, required: image <=10000)").replace("  if photo != null:","  insert photos {parent:p.id,kind:'checkout',photo:required}\n  if photo != null:");assert!(!has(&sibling,"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE"));
}
#[test]fn unknown_child_filters_and_a_different_parent_do_not_establish_an_upload_obligation(){

 let unknown=format!("{base}invariant parents always exists photos[parent = .id, kind = 'checkout']\n");assert!(!has(&unknown,"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE"));
 let other=format!("{}invariant parents always exists photos[parent = .id]\n",CHILD.replace("add(photo: image <=10000 ?)","add(photo: image <=10000 ?, other:parents)").replace("parent:p.id","parent:other"));assert!(!has(&other,"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE"));
}

 for sibling in ["  for image in photos:\n    insert photos {parent:p.id,photo:image}\n", "  extra = (insert photos {parent:p.id,photo:required}) if enabled else null\n"]{
  let s=base.replace("  if photo != null:",&format!("{sibling}  if photo != null:"));let s=format!("{s}invariant parents always exists photos[parent = .id]\n");assert!(has(&s,"N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE"));
 }
}

#[test]fn billing_asymmetry_names_the_private_column_and_its_owner_guard(){
 let s=include_str!("security/wave23/042.q");let r=notes(s);let ns=r.notes.iter().filter(|n|n.code=="N_PRIVATE_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY").collect::<Vec<_>>();assert_eq!(ns.len(),1,"{:?}",r.notes);

 assert!(!r.notes.iter().any(|n|n.code=="N_INSERT_WRITE_INTENT"&&n.message.contains("agencyBillingNotes")));
 assert!(!r.notes.iter().any(|n|n.code.contains("CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY")&&n.message.contains("signings.fee")));
 assert!(security::note_priority(&ns[0].code)<security::note_priority("N_INSERT_WRITE_INTENT"));
 let guarded=s.replace("  return insert signings {", "  require exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner'] else OWNER\n  return insert signings {");assert!(!has(&guarded,"N_PRIVATE_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY"));
 let literal=s.replace("fee, agencyBillingNotes, outOfState:","fee, agencyBillingNotes:null, outOfState:");assert!(!has(&literal,"N_PRIVATE_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY"));
}
#[test]fn mixed_other_row_and_intervening_write_guards_do_not_become_this_columns_guard(){
 let s=include_str!("security/wave23/042.q");
 let mixed=s.replace("if agencyBillingNotes != s.agencyBillingNotes:","if agencyBillingNotes != s.agencyBillingNotes and fee > 1:");
 let other=s.replace("mutation updateBilling(signing: signings,", "mutation updateBilling(signing: signings, other: signings,").replace("  if agencyBillingNotes != s.agencyBillingNotes:","  otherRow = signings[other] ! else MISSING\n  if agencyBillingNotes != otherRow.agencyBillingNotes:");
 let intervening=s.replace("  update signings[signing] { fee, agencyBillingNotes }","  update signings[signing] { fee }\n  update signings[signing] { fee, agencyBillingNotes }");
 let early=s.replace("  if agencyBillingNotes != s.agencyBillingNotes:\n    require exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner']", "  if agencyBillingNotes = s.agencyBillingNotes:\n    return null\n  require exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner']");

//! Mutable syntax traversal shared by planning and final index-name resolution.
use crate::ast::*;

pub(crate) fn expression(value: &mut Expr, visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    match value {
        Expr::Read(read) | Expr::Exists { read, .. } | Expr::Created { read, .. } => read_children(read, visit),
        Expr::Unary { value, .. } | Expr::Member { base: value, .. } | Expr::Assert { value, .. } | Expr::ShapeUse { base: value, .. } => expression(value, visit),
        Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => { expression(left, visit); expression(right, visit); }
        Expr::Array { items, .. } | Expr::Call { args: items, .. } => { for item in items { expression(item, visit); } }
        Expr::Object { fields, .. } => { for field in fields { if let Some(value) = &mut field.value { expression(value, visit); } } }
        Expr::Shape { base, fields, .. } => { expression(base, visit); shape(fields, visit); }
        Expr::If { then, condition, otherwise, .. } => { expression(then, visit); expression(condition, visit); expression(otherwise, visit); }
        Expr::Walk { seed, .. } => expression(seed, visit),
        Expr::Write { keys, fields, condition, .. } => {
            for key in keys { expression(&mut key.value, visit); }
            for field in fields { if let Some(value) = &mut field.value { expression(value, visit); } }
            if let Some(value) = condition { expression(value, visit); }
        }
        Expr::Merge(merge) => {
            for value in [&mut merge.matching, &mut merge.filter, &mut merge.cursor].into_iter().flatten() { expression(value, visit); }
            let mut lanes = Vec::new();
            for mut lane in std::mem::take(&mut merge.lanes) {
                if let Some(predicate) = &mut lane.admission { expression(predicate, visit); }
                if let Some((_, read)) = &mut lane.nulls {
                    let mut value = Expr::Read(*read.clone());
                    expression(&mut value, visit);
                    let Expr::Read(planned) = value else { unreachable!("planned null scan") };
                    **read = planned;
                }
                if let Some(value) = &mut lane.distinct { expression(value, visit); }
                if let Some(fields) = &mut lane.project { shape(fields, visit); }
                for value in &mut lane.prefix { expression(value, visit); }
                if let Some((_, value)) = &mut lane.loop_ { expression(value, visit); }
                if let Some(read) = &mut lane.planned {
                    let mut value = Expr::Read(*read.clone());
                    expression(&mut value, visit);
                    match value {
                        Expr::Read(planned) => **read = planned,
                        Expr::Merge(nested) if lane.loop_.is_none() => {
                            for mut child in nested.lanes {
                                child.branch = lane.branch.clone();
                                child.project = lane.project.clone();
                                lanes.push(child);
                            }
                            continue;
                        }
                        _ => unreachable!("a planned lane is a read or an expanded read"),
                    }
                }
                lanes.push(lane);
            }
            merge.lanes = lanes;
        }
        Expr::Name { .. } | Expr::Focal { .. } | Expr::Literal { .. } => {}
    }
    visit(value);
}

fn read_children(read: &mut ReadExpr, visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    for key in &mut read.keys { expression(&mut key.value, visit); }
    for seek in &mut read.seeks { expression(&mut seek.value, visit); }
    for value in [&mut read.filter, &mut read.matching].into_iter().flatten() { expression(value, visit); }
    if let Some(value) = read.range.as_mut().and_then(|range| range.cursor.as_mut()) { expression(value, visit); }
}

pub(super) fn shape(fields: &mut [ShapeField], visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    for field in fields { if let ShapeField::Field { value, .. } = field { expression(value, visit); } }
}

pub(super) fn statements(body: &mut [Stmt], visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    for stmt in body {
        match stmt {
            Stmt::Bind { value, .. } | Stmt::Expr { value, .. } | Stmt::Return { value, .. } => expression(value, visit),
            Stmt::Require { condition, .. } => expression(condition, visit),
            Stmt::Emit { payload, once, .. } => {
                expression(payload, visit);
                if let Some(key) = once { expression(key, visit); }
            }
            Stmt::If { condition, then_body, else_body, .. } => { expression(condition, visit); statements(then_body, visit); statements(else_body, visit); }
            Stmt::As { principal: domain, body, .. } | Stmt::For { domain, body, .. } => { expression(domain, visit); statements(body, visit); }
            Stmt::Break { .. } | Stmt::Continue { .. } => {}
        }
    }
}

pub(crate) fn document(doc: &mut Document, visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#8-wave-21-release-evolution — historical lookups receive the same predicate/index planning as ordinary reads.
    for fill in doc.backfills.iter_mut().chain(&mut doc.downfills) {expression(&mut fill.value,visit);}

    for program in &mut doc.programs { statements(&mut program.prelude, visit); statements(&mut program.body, visit); }
    for function in &mut doc.functions {
        match &mut function.body { FunctionBody::Expression(value) => expression(value, visit), FunctionBody::Block(body) => statements(body, visit) }
    }
    for decl in &mut doc.shapes { shape(&mut decl.fields, visit); }
}
//! Narrow stable leading-require reduction for operation-specific authority.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#81-exact-conservative-boundary-review-clarification — unknown write sets never establish stability.
use super::*;
use crate::ast::{Stmt,ProgramDecl,ProgramKind,InvariantDecl,RuleDecl};
use super::super::catalog::TableInfo;
pub(super) fn resolve<'a>(catalog:&'a Catalog,decl:&InvariantDecl)->Option<(String,&'a ProgramDecl)>{
    if matches!(decl.target.as_str(),"read"|"insert"|"update"|"delete"|"always"|"write"){return None;}
    let matches=catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|{let module=std::path::Path::new(&d.file).file_stem().unwrap().to_string_lossy();d.programs.iter().map(move|p|(super::super::operation_name(&module,&p.name),p))})
        .filter(|(name,p)|p.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation&&(name==&decl.target||p.name==decl.target)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if matches.len()==1 {matches.into_iter().next()}else{None}
}
pub(super) fn safe(rule:&RuleExpr)->bool {match rule{
    RuleExpr::Exists{key,equalities,..}=>key.iter().chain(equalities.iter().map(|(_,t)|t)).all(|t|matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_))),
    RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>xs.iter().all(safe),
    RuleExpr::Eq(a,b)|RuleExpr::Ne(a,b)=>[a,b].iter().all(|t|matches!(t,RuleTerm::Viewer|RuleTerm::Literal(_))),
    RuleExpr::Allow|RuleExpr::Deny|RuleExpr::Public{..}=>true,_=>false,
}}
pub(super) fn requirements(catalog:&Catalog,info:&TableInfo,p:&ProgramDecl)->Vec<RuleExpr>{
    let mut out=Vec::new();
    for s in p.prelude.iter().chain(&p.body){let Stmt::Require{condition,site,..}=s else{break};
        let decl=RuleDecl{effect:ast::Effect::Read,bindings:Vec::new(),value:condition.clone(),site:site.clone()};
        if let Ok(rule)=super::super::schema::bind_rule(catalog,info,&decl)&&safe(&rule){out.push(rule);}
    }out
}
pub(super) fn prove(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant)->Result<bool,String>{
    let p=bound.programs.iter().find(|p|Some(&p.program.name)==inv.operation.as_ref()).ok_or("operation did not bind")?;
    if !safe(inv.policy.as_ref().ok_or("unsupported policy")?){return Err("operation policy needs unsupported target-image, negative-probe or live-reference bindings".into());}
    let writes=snapback4_core::ir::write_tables(&p.program);
    if !writes.iter().any(|(t,_)|t==&inv.table){return Err("operation has no direct write to the named table; implicit effects require a reduction".into());}
    let dependencies=inv.requirements.iter().flat_map(kernel::dependencies).collect::<BTreeSet<_>>();
    if writes.iter().any(|(t,_)|dependencies.contains(t)) {return Err("require dependency can be changed by this operation, including its target write".into());}
    if bound.schema.tables.values().any(|t|!t.maintained.is_empty()||!t.sequences.is_empty()||t.on_delete.values().any(|a|a.writes())) || !bound.schema.maintains.is_empty(){return Err("implicit maintain/sequence/referential writes require dependency stability reduction".into());}
    // Source helpers/control flow may hide write sets or viewer changes.
    let json=serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap();
    fn opaque(v:&serde_json::Value)->bool{match v{serde_json::Value::Object(m)=>m.keys().any(|k|matches!(k.as_str(),"For"|"If"|"JobAs"|"JobQuery"|"Emit"))||m.values().any(opaque),serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>xs.iter().any(opaque),_=>false}}
    if opaque(&json){return Err("operation control flow or opaque effects are outside stable leading-require reduction".into());}
    let admission=RuleExpr::And(inv.requirements.clone());
    // Table-rule gates can only further restrict a dominating stable require;
    // using the require alone overapproximates effective owner-field gates.
    kernel::implies(&admission,inv.policy.as_ref().unwrap())
}

invariant trucks.read only .owner = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
invariant trucks.insert only exists roster[viewer, role = 'operator']

-- Each application must include interior photos, the fire extinguisher tag
-- photo and the permit fee: all three columns are required, so an
-- application row cannot exist without them.
table applications:
  truck: trucks
  owner: principal
  interiorPhotos: [image <=5000000] 1..4
  extinguisherTag: image <=5000000
  feeCents: int 1..100000000
  status: enum('applied', 'inspected', 'inspection recorded', 'approved', 'failed') default 'applied'
  failedAt: time default now
  lastPassed: bool default false
  createdAt: time default now
  immutable truck, owner, interiorPhotos, extinguisherTag, feeCents, createdAt
  read <- .owner = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  insert <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'operator']
  update <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  online only

mutation submitApplication(truck: trucks, interiorPhotos: [image <=5000000] 1..4, extinguisherTag: image <=5000000, feeCents: int 1..100000000):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'operator'] else NOT_OPERATOR
  t = trucks[truck] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require t.owner = viewer else NOT_YOUR_TRUCK
  flag = operatorFlags[viewer]
  require flag = null or flag.suspended = false else OPERATOR_SUSPENDED
  return insert applications { truck, owner: viewer, interiorPhotos, extinguisherTag, feeCents }

query myApplications(c: cursor ?):
  return applications where .owner = viewer first 50 by .createdAt after c

-- The live board: every application as it moves applied -> inspection recorded ->
-- approved / failed. Staff only.
query board(c: cursor ?):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_STAFF
  return applications first 50 by .createdAt after c

invariant applications.read only .owner = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
invariant applications.insert only exists roster[viewer, role = 'operator']

-- v2 renamed the 'inspected' stage to 'inspection recorded'. The column still
-- admits the old value so that every historical row survives adoption and
-- rollback without dormancy; the migrateInspectionRecorded mutation below
-- moves every remaining 'inspected' row to the renamed stage (run after
-- deploy and after every forward), and no workflow mutation writes the old
-- value anymore. Rollback to a release without the renamed value projects
-- 'inspection recorded' back to 'inspected' instead of hiding rows.
down applications inspectedView(status: enum('applied', 'inspected', 'inspection recorded', 'approved', 'failed')): {status: 'inspected' if status = 'inspection recorded' else status}

-- One-shot data migration for the v2 rename. Supervisor-only. Moves a single
-- remaining 'inspected' application to 'inspection recorded'; callers page
-- the board until none remain.
mutation migrateInspectionRecorded(application: applications):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_SUPERVISOR
  app = applications[application] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require app.status = 'inspected' else NOT_INSPECTED
  update applications[app.id] { status: 'inspection recorded' }
  return applications[app.id]

-- Inspections are written by inspectors (or supervisors). owner is the truck
-- owner's principal, denormalized so operators read their own reports and
-- staff read everything.
table inspections:
  truck: trucks
  application: applications ?
  permit: permits ?
  inspector: principal
  passed: bool
  notes: text <=2000 ?
  kind: enum('initial', 'reinspection', 'routine')
  inspectedAt: time default now
  owner: principal
  immutable truck, application, permit, inspector, passed, notes, kind, inspectedAt, owner
  read <- .owner = viewer or exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  insert <- exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor']
  online only

-- First inspection of an application: applied -> inspection recorded.
mutation inspectApplication(application: applications, passed: bool, notes: text <=2000 ?):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_INSPECTOR
  app = applications[application] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require app.status = 'applied' else NOT_APPLIED
  i = insert inspections { truck: app.truck, application: app.id, inspector: viewer, passed, notes, kind: 'initial', owner: app.owner }
  update applications[app.id] { status: 'inspection recorded', lastPassed: passed }
  return i

-- Reinspection of a failed application, only within ten days of the failure.
mutation reinspectApplication(application: applications, passed: bool, notes: text <=2000 ?):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_INSPECTOR
  app = applications[application] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require app.status = 'failed' else NOT_FAILED
  require now <= app.failedAt + 10d else REINSPECTION_LATE
  i = insert inspections { truck: app.truck, application: app.id, inspector: viewer, passed, notes, kind: 'reinspection', owner: app.owner }
  update applications[app.id] { status: 'inspection recorded', lastPassed: passed }
  return i

-- Decision on an inspection-recorded application. Approval issues the permit, valid
-- 365 days; rejection marks it failed and starts the ten-day reinspection
-- window.
mutation approveApplication(application: applications):
  require exists roster[viewer, role = 'inspector'] or exists roster[viewer, role = 'supervisor'] else NOT_INSPECTOR
  app = applications[application] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require app.status = 'inspection recorded' else NOT_INSPECTED
  require app.lastPassed else DECISION_CONFLICTS
  p = insert permits { truck: app.truck, owner: app.owner, application: app.id, issuedAt: now, expiresAt: now + 365d, status: 'active', flagged: false, failedAt: now }
  update applications[app.id] { status: 'approved' }
  update trucks[app.truck] { activePermit: p.id }
  return p
  require exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'title_company'] else NOT_TITLE_COMPANY
  return insert signings { titleCompany: viewer, signerName, address, documents, idScan, fee, agencyBillingNotes, outOfState: outOfState ?? false }

-- Dispatchers and the owner assign a registered notary. Out-of-state papers
-- need an owner-approved notary.
mutation assignSigning(signing: signings, notary: principal):
  require exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'dispatcher'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_STAFF 'Only dispatch staff assign signings.'
  require exists roles[principal = notary, role = 'notary'] else NOT_A_NOTARY 'That principal is not a notary.'
  s = signings[signing] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require s.status = 'requested' else BAD_STATUS 'Only requested signings can be assigned.'
  if s.outOfState:
    p = notaryProfiles[principal = notary] ! else NOT_APPROVED 'That notary is not approved for out-of-state papers.'
    require p.approvedOutOfState else NOT_APPROVED 'That notary is not approved for out-of-state papers.'
  update signings[signing] { notary, status: 'assigned' }
  return null

-- The assigned notary (or dispatch staff) records the signing.
mutation markSigned(signing: signings):
  s = signings[signing] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require s.status = 'assigned' else BAD_STATUS 'Only assigned signings can be marked signed.'
  require s.notary = viewer or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'dispatcher'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_ASSIGNED 'Only the assigned notary or dispatch staff records the signing.'
  update signings[signing] { status: 'signed', signedAt: now }
  return null

-- Signed papers ship back within one day of signing; later returns refuse.
mutation markReturned(signing: signings):
  s = signings[signing] ! else NOT_FOUND
  require s.status = 'signed' else BAD_STATUS 'Only signed signings can be marked returned.'
  require s.signedAt != null else BAD_STATUS 'This signing has no signing time.'
  require now <= s.signedAt + 24h else RETURN_OVERDUE 'Signed papers must ship back within one day of signing.'
  require s.notary = viewer or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'dispatcher'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_ASSIGNED 'Only the assigned notary or dispatch staff records the return.'
  update signings[signing] { status: 'returned', returnedAt: now }
  return null

-- Dispatch staff adjust the fee charged to the title company. Only the
-- agency owner may change the agency billing notes; the dispatcher may
-- still read them but cannot change them.
mutation updateBilling(signing: signings, fee: money USD, agencyBillingNotes: text <=2000 ? alias billingNotes):
  require exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'dispatcher'] or exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_STAFF 'Only dispatch staff edits billing.'
  s = signings[signing] ! else NOT_FOUND
  if agencyBillingNotes != s.agencyBillingNotes:
    require exists roles[principal = viewer, role = 'owner'] else NOT_OWNER_NOTES 'Only the agency owner changes agency billing notes.'
  update signings[signing] { fee, agencyBillingNotes }
  return null

-- Live dispatch board: every caller sees exactly the rows (and columns)
index, align the source write's named/positional key expressions with that bound
index's columns and merge the implicit stored fields with its explicit payload.
Keep the existing source pin resolver as a fallback for unambiguous source forms.
No planner/runtime rule or authorization changes: this repairs advice/gate recall
for the already documented shape. A two-unique-index recipient-key regression
and a reader that rechecks the role pin the gate and its suppression. Re-measure
the corpus for any newly recognized gates and report their individual review cost.

### 16.9 Day-two precision and destructive evidence questions

Implementer: Codex for Charlie Cheever, 2026-10-22; commissioned by the wave-23
day-two addendum. This supersedes the upload shape and stock-template disposition
in §§16.2/16.5/16.7 and narrows stale-creation advice. It changes analysis and
finite witness selection, never runtime admission or application authority.

**Optional uploads.** Optional arguments and optional columns are a valid model,
including deliberate v2 loosening and exceptions such as a lockout without proof.
Do not gate them merely because their names suggest evidence. Gate an optional
image/file argument flowing to a creating media column only when that column is
required, or a direct positive conjunct of an authored `table always` invariant
requires that exact column to be non-null. Flatten conjunctions and recognize
`field != null` and the exactly equivalent `not (field = null)` in either operand
order. Other negation, an OR, an unrelated column, comment, product document or unknown predicate is not
a mandatory-media declaration. No natural-language specification parsing, deploy
diff exemption, lane exemption or new intent language is introduced. Required
child media with optional arguments does not, by itself, require a child row.
A child-local non-null branch is valid optional attachment creation, including
040's return photo, and cannot fail check on that basis. Gate missing parent
evidence only when an independent parent `always exists child[...]` conjunct
pins the exact parent-reference relationship and the operation creates that parent.
Such a child-only guard cannot satisfy the explicit parent-level obligation.
Match the exact created parent reference and every additional literal child-probe
filter (e.g. checkout vs return). Unknown filters do not establish this gate.
An unconditional ordinary sibling insertion satisfying that same probe discharges
the obligation; an unrelated optional attachment must not be gated for it.
Conditional or if-absent sibling insertions cannot establish that discharge.
Without that obligation, parent-plus-conditional-child creation may ask the existing
advisory optional-evidence question, but never imposes an acknowledgment.
 Existing dominating non-null
guards across every creating context still suppress the question. Required scalar media (or a required nonempty list of required media)
with required arguments needs no optional-upload review. A required list allowing
zero elements is not mandatory evidence. Existing generic optional
evidence advice remains advisory. Bump the analyzer revision; remove the stock
social disposition and its special injection/custody code, because intended
text-only messages now check without any acknowledgment, including after edits.
Keep individual source-bound reviews for the two genuine gated shapes.

**Stale eligibility.** Match a creator's dominating condition on a non-role,
non-immutable column that another authored mutation actually changes (or an
existence condition on a deletable dependency). A mere read of an immutable owner
or an unrelated write to that table is not mutable eligibility. A later operation
qualifies only when it writes an approval-like literal enum state (`approved`,
`accepted`, `authorized`, `granted`, `issued`, `active`, `confirmed`, `paid`,
`signed`) or a true approval/authorization flag, and updates the created table
or creates a directly referencing child. An arbitrary edit, inspection, rejection,
cleanup or status-copy is outside this name-based advisory fragment. Rechecking
the relevant eligibility column (or named existence dependency) suppresses that
path; merely reading an immutable owner or a different column cannot suppress it. Group all creator and later-write
sites into one question per created table and mutable dependency; use the
lexicographically least (filename, numeric line, numeric column) decision site,
with sorted creator/decision names and sites plus the eligibility columns. This is a question, not an assertion that
every such decision must repeat the check; unsupported forms stay uncovered.

**Create/edit asymmetry.** Add `N_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY`, ranked with likely-hole
questions (priority 2), and `N_PRIVATE_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY` at priority 1
for a write-only private payload. Compare

    17	    let changes=if column.is_some()&&changes::prior_effects(&expr){&[][..]}else{changes};
    18	    super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut expr,&mut |e|match e{
    19	        Expr::Write{..}=>{let mut context=p.clone();context.prelude=Vec::new();context.body=guards.to_vec();if let Some(column)=column{for change in changes{if change.matches(e,column){context.body.extend(change.requirements.clone());}}}out.push((e.clone(),context));},
    20	        Expr::Call{name,args,..} if depth<32=>if let Some(f)=catalog.functions.get(name){
    21	            let mut locals=f.args.iter().zip(args).map(|(arg,value)|(arg.name.clone(),value.clone())).collect();
    22	            let mut inherited=guards.to_vec();
    23	            match &f.body{
    24	                FunctionBody::Expression(value)=>expression(catalog,p,value,&locals,&inherited,changes,column,depth+1,out),
    25	                FunctionBody::Block(body)=>statements(catalog,p,body,&mut locals,&mut inherited,&mut changes.to_vec(),column,depth+1,out),
    26	            }
    27	        },_=>{}
    28	    });
    29	}
    30	// Only an unconditional direct call exports a helper's guaranteed prefix.
    31	// Conditional/loop-local requirements never become caller assumptions.
    32	fn helper_guards(catalog:&Catalog,expr:&Expr,env:&Env,depth:usize)->Vec<Stmt>{
    33	    if depth>=32{return Vec::new();}
    34	    let Expr::Call{name,args,..}=resolve(expr,env)else{return Vec::new();};
    35	    let Some(f)=catalog.functions.get(&name)else{return Vec::new();};
    36	    let mut locals=f.args.iter().zip(args).map(|(a,v)|(a.name.clone(),v)).collect::<Env>();
    37	    let FunctionBody::Block(body)=&f.body else{return if let FunctionBody::Expression(value)=&f.body{helper_guards(catalog,value,&locals,depth+1)}else{Vec::new()};};
    38	    let mut out=Vec::new();
    39	    for s in body{match s{
    40	        Stmt::Require{condition,code,message,site}=>out.push(Stmt::Require{condition:resolve(condition,&locals),code:code.clone(),message:message.clone(),site:site.clone()}),
    41	        Stmt::Bind{names,value,..}=>{out.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,&locals,depth+1));if names.len()==1{locals.insert(names[0].clone(),resolve(value,&locals));}},
    42	        Stmt::Expr{value,..}=>out.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,&locals,depth+1)),
    43	        Stmt::Return{value,..}=>{out.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,&locals,depth+1));break;},_=>break,
    44	    }}out
    45	}
    46	fn statements(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl,body:&[Stmt],env:&mut Env,guards:&mut Vec<Stmt>,changes:&mut Vec<Change>,column:Option<&str>,depth:usize,out:&mut Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>){
    47	    for s in body{
    48	        let consuming=column.is_some()&&changes::effectful(s);
    49	        match s{
    50	        Stmt::Bind{names,value,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,value,env,guards,changes,column,depth,out);guards.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,env,0));if names.len()==1{env.insert(names[0].clone(),resolve(value,env));}},
    51	        Stmt::Require{condition,code,message,site}=>{
    52	            expression(catalog,p,condition,env,guards,changes,column,depth,out);
    53	            guards.push(Stmt::Require{condition:resolve(condition,env),code:code.clone(),message:message.clone(),site:site.clone()});
    54	        },
    55	        Stmt::Return{value,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,value,env,guards,changes,column,depth,out);break;},
    56	        Stmt::Expr{value,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,value,env,guards,changes,column,depth,out);guards.extend(helper_guards(catalog,value,env,0));},
    57	        Stmt::Emit{payload,once,..}=>{
    58	            if column.is_some(){changes.clear();}
    59	            expression(catalog,p,payload,env,guards,changes,column,depth,out);
    60	            if let Some(once)=once{expression(catalog,p,once,env,guards,changes,column,depth,out);}
    61	        },
    62	        Stmt::If{condition,then_body,else_body,site}=>{
    63	            if column.is_some()&&changes::expression_effectful(&resolve(condition,env)){changes.clear();}
    64	            expression(catalog,p,condition,env,guards,changes,column,depth,out);
    65	            let condition=resolve(condition,env);
    66	            let mut then_guards=guards.clone();
    67	            then_guards.push(Stmt::Require{condition:condition.clone(),code:"$advice".into(),message:None,site:site.clone()});
    68	            statements(catalog,p,then_body,&mut env.clone(),&mut then_guards,&mut changes.clone(),column,depth,out);
    69	            let mut else_guards=guards.clone();
    70	            else_guards.push(Stmt::Require{condition:Expr::Unary{op:"not".into(),value:Box::new(condition.clone()),site:site.clone()},code:"$advice".into(),message:None,site:site.clone()});
    71	            statements(catalog,p,else_body,&mut env.clone(),&mut else_guards,&mut changes.clone(),column,depth,out);
    72	            if column.is_some(){changes.extend(changes::after(catalog,&condition,then_body,else_body,env));}
    73	        },
    74	        Stmt::For{domain,name,body,..}=>{expression(catalog,p,domain,env,guards,changes,column,depth,out);let mut local=env.clone();local.remove(name);statements(catalog,p,body,&mut local,&mut guards.clone(),&mut changes.clone(),column,depth,out);},
    75	        Stmt::As{body,..}=>statements(catalog,p,body,&mut env.clone(),&mut guards.clone(),&mut changes.clone(),column,depth,out),_=>{}
    76	    }
    77	    if consuming{changes.clear();}
    78	    }
    79	}
    80	pub(super) fn writes(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl)->Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>{walk_writes(catalog,p,None)}
    81	pub(super) fn column_writes(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl,column:&str)->Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>{walk_writes(catalog,p,Some(column))}
    82	fn walk_writes(catalog:&Catalog,p:&ProgramDecl,column:Option<&str>)->Vec<(Expr,ProgramDecl)>{
    83	    let mut out=Vec::new();let mut env=Env::new();let mut guards=Vec::new();let mut changes=Vec::new();
    84	    statements(catalog,p,&p.prelude,&mut env,&mut guards,&mut changes,column,0,&mut out);
    85	    statements(catalog,p,&p.body,&mut env,&mut guards,&mut changes,column,0,&mut out);out
    86	}
     1	//! Changed-column guards are advisory and bind the exact row, field and new value.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#169-day-two-precision-and-destructive-evidence-questions — no table-wide promotion of a column condition.
     3	use super::*;
     4	#[derive(Clone)]
     5	pub(super) struct Change{old:Expr,new:Expr,pub requirements:Vec<Stmt>}
     6	fn read(e:&Expr)->Option<&ast::ReadExpr>{match e{Expr::Read(r)=>Some(r),Expr::Assert{value,..}=>read(value),_=>None}}
     7	impl Change{
     8	    pub fn matches(&self,write:&Expr,column:&str)->bool{
     9	        let Expr::Write{op:ast::WriteOp::Update,table,keys,fields,..}=write else{return false;};
    10	        let Expr::Member{base,name,..}=&self.old else{return false;};let Some(old)=read(base)else{return false;};
    11	        name==column&&old.table==*table&&old.keys.len()==keys.len()&&old.keys.iter().zip(keys).all(|(a,b)|a.column==b.column&&crate::print::expression(&a.value)==crate::print::expression(&b.value))&&fields.iter().any(|f|f.name==column&&f.value.as_ref().is_some_and(|v|crate::print::expression(v)==crate::print::expression(&self.new)))
    12	    }
    13	}
    14	// Unknown calls and writes end the pending interval; pure leading helper
    15	// requirements can be recovered only inside the immediately tested branch.
    16	pub(super) fn expression_effectful(e:&Expr)->bool{
    17	    let mut e=e.clone();let mut unknown=false;super::super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut e,&mut |e|if matches!(e,Expr::Write{..}|Expr::Call{..}){unknown=true;});unknown
    18	}
    19	pub(super) fn prior_effects(e:&Expr)->bool{
    20	    let mut e=e.clone();let(mut calls,mut writes)=(false,0);
    21	    super::super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut e,&mut |e|match e{Expr::Write{..}=>writes+=1,Expr::Call{..}=>calls=true,_=>{}});
    22	    calls||writes>1
    23	}
    24	pub(super) fn effectful(s:&Stmt)->bool{match s{
    25	    Stmt::Expr{value,..}|Stmt::Bind{value,..}|Stmt::Return{value,..}|Stmt::Require{condition:value,..}=>expression_effectful(value),
    26	    Stmt::If{condition,then_body,else_body,..}=>expression_effectful(condition)||then_body.iter().chain(else_body).any(effectful),
    27	    Stmt::For{..}|Stmt::As{..}|Stmt::Emit{..}=>true,_=>false,
    28	}}
    29	
    30	fn prefix(_catalog:&Catalog,body:&[Stmt],env:&Env)->Vec<Stmt>{
    31	    let mut env=env.clone();let mut out=Vec::new();
    32	    for s in body{match s{
    33	        Stmt::Require{condition,code,message,site}=>out.push(Stmt::Require{condition:resolve(condition,&env),code:code.clone(),message:message.clone(),site:site.clone()}),
    34	        Stmt::Bind{names,value,..}=>{if effectful(s){break;}if names.len()==1{env.insert(names[0].clone(),resolve(value,&env));}},
    35	        _=>break,
    36	    }}out
    37	}
    38	pub(super) fn after(catalog:&Catalog,condition:&Expr,then_body:&[Stmt],else_body:&[Stmt],env:&Env)->Vec<Change>{
    39	    if expression_effectful(condition){return Vec::new();}
    40	    let Expr::Binary{op,left,right,..}=condition else{return Vec::new();};
    41	    let body=match op.as_str(){"!="=>then_body,"="=>else_body,_=>return Vec::new()};
    42	    if body.iter().any(|s|matches!(s,Stmt::Return{..})||effectful(s)){return Vec::new();}
    43	    let requirements=prefix(catalog,body,env);if requirements.is_empty(){return Vec::new();}
    44	    [(left,right),(right,left)].into_iter().filter(|(old,_)|matches!(old.as_ref(),Expr::Member{..})).map(|(old,new)|Change{old:*old.clone(),new:*new.clone(),requirements:requirements.clone()}).collect()
    45	}
//! Real operation histories from a complete trusted fixture, including assets.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#122-reproducible-campaigns-and-reachability — only actual fixture traces establish application reachability.
use super::*;
const LIMIT:usize=512;
#[derive(Clone,Debug,Serialize,serde::Deserialize)]
pub struct Step {pub operation:String,pub viewer:String,pub args:BTreeMap<String,Value>,pub result:serde_json::Value}

pub(super) fn viewers(bound:&Bound)->Vec<String>{
    let mut out=BTreeSet::from([VIEWER.into(),OTHER.into(),"account:security-no-role".into(),"service:security".into()]);
    out.extend(bound.personas.iter().map(|p|if p.contains(':'){p.clone()}else{format!("dev:{p}")}));
    fn seats(value:&serde_json::Value,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match value{
        serde_json::Value::String(s) if s.starts_with("service:")&&s.len()>8=>{out.insert(s.clone());},
        serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>for x in xs{seats(x,out)},
        serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>for x in xs.values(){seats(x,out)},_=>{}
    }}
    seats(&serde_json::to_value(&bound.schema).unwrap(),&mut out);
    for p in &bound.programs{seats(&serde_json::to_value(&p.program).unwrap(),&mut out);}
    seats(&serde_json::to_value(&bound.invariants).unwrap(),&mut out);
    out.into_iter().collect()
}
pub(super) fn actors(bound:&Bound,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>)->Vec<String>{
    let mut out=viewers(bound);
    for (table,rs) in rows{for row in rs{for (c,k) in &bound.schema.tables[table].columns{
        if matches!(k.required(),C::Principal)&&let Ok(Value::Id(p))=row.field(c)&&!out.contains(p){out.push(p.clone());}
    }}}
    out
}
pub(super) fn fixture_cases(bound:&Bound,p:&Program,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String],out:&mut Campaign)->Vec<BTreeMap<String,Value>>{
    let args=&p.args;
    let mut literals=BTreeMap::<String,Vec<Value>>::new();
    fn collect(value:&serde_json::Value,out:&mut BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>){
        if let Some(binary)=value.get("Binary") {for (a,b) in [("left","right"),("right","left")] {
            if let (Some(name),Some(literal))=(binary.get(a).and_then(|v|v.get("Variable")).and_then(serde_json::Value::as_str),binary.get(b).and_then(|v|v.get("Literal"))) {
                if let Ok(value)=serde_json::from_value::<Value>(literal.clone()){out.entry(name.into()).or_default().push(value);}
            }
        }}
        match value{serde_json::Value::Object(xs)=>for v in xs.values(){collect(v,out)},serde_json::Value::Array(xs)=>for v in xs{collect(v,out)},_=>{}}
    }
    collect(&serde_json::to_value(p).unwrap(),&mut literals);
    fn values(name:&str,k:&ir::ArgType,rows:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,actors:&[String])->Option<Vec<Value>>{
        Some(match k{
            ir::ArgType::Enum{variants}=>variants.iter().map(|s|Value::String(s.clone())).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Text{min,max,..}=>['z','y'].into_iter().map(|c|Value::String(c.to_string().repeat((*min).max(1).min(*max) as usize))).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Int=>vec![Value::Int(0),Value::Int(1),Value::Int(2)],
            ir::ArgType::IntRange{min,max}=>{let mut xs=vec![*min,1i64.clamp(*min,*max),*max];xs.sort();xs.dedup();xs.into_iter().map(Value::Int).collect()},
            ir::ArgType::Bool=>vec![Value::Bool(false),Value::Bool(true)],
            ir::ArgType::Time=>vec![Value::Int(NOW-86_400_000),Value::Int(NOW),Value::Int(NOW+86_400_000)],
            ir::ArgType::Principal=>actors.iter().map(|s|Value::Id(s.clone())).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Ref{table} if rows.get(table).is_some_and(|rs|!rs.is_empty())=>rows[table].iter().filter_map(|r|r.field("id").ok().cloned()).collect(),
            ir::ArgType::Optional(inner)=>{let mut v=vec![Value::Null];v.extend(values(name,inner,rows,actors)?);v},
            _=>vec![arguments(&BTreeMap::from([(name.into(),k.clone())]),0)?.remove(name)?],
        })
    }
    let mut cases=vec![BTreeMap::new()];
    for (name,k) in args{
        let Some(mut vals)=values(name,k,rows,actors)else{out.unsupported.insert(format!("{name}: history needs a typed asset argument"));return Vec::new();};
        for literal in literals.get(name).into_iter().flatten() {
            let valid=match (k,literal){
                (ir::ArgType::Text{min,max,..},Value::String(s))=>s.chars().filter(|c|!c.is_whitespace()).count()>=*min as usize&&s.chars().count()<=*max as usize,
                (ir::ArgType::Int,Value::Int(_))=>true,
                (ir::ArgType::IntRange{min,max},Value::Int(n))=>(*min..=*max).contains(n),_=>false,
            };
            if valid&&!vals.contains(literal){vals.insert(0,literal.clone());}
        }
        let mut next=Vec::new();
        'product:for offset in 0..vals.len(){for n in 0..cases.len().max(vals.len()){
            if out.exhausted(){return next;}
            let mut case=cases[n%cases.len()].clone();case.insert(name.clone(),vals[(n+offset)%vals.len()].clone());
            if !next.contains(&case){next.push(case);}if next.len()==LIMIT{out.unsupported.insert("history arguments capped at 512 combinations".into());break 'product;}
        }}
        cases=next;
    }
    let focused=super::executed::write_arguments(bound,p).into_iter().filter(|a|matches!(p.args.get(a),Some(ir::ArgType::Optional(_)))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if focused.is_empty(){return cases;}
    let mut prioritized=Vec::new();
    for case in cases{
        for name in &focused{if let Some(value)=arguments(&BTreeMap::from([(name.clone(),p.args[name].clone())]),4).and_then(|mut a|a.remove(name))&&value!=Value::Null{
            let mut alternate=case.clone();alternate.insert(name.clone(),value);if !prioritized.contains(&alternate){prioritized.push(alternate);}
        }}
        if !prioritized.contains(&case){prioritized.push(case.clone());}
        if prioritized.len()>=LIMIT{prioritized.truncate(LIMIT);out.unsupported.insert("column-write argument frontier capped at 512; remaining combinations uncovered".into());break;}
    }prioritized
}
// Preserve status/decision alternatives on each resource tuple before the
// small directed cap; text/noise arguments must not correlate bools with ids.
fn directed_cases(p:&Program,cases:Vec<BTreeMap<String,Value>>,campaign:&mut Campaign)->Vec<BTreeMap<String,Value>>{
    let identities=p.args.iter().filter(|(_,k)|matches!(k,ir::ArgType::Ref{..}|ir::ArgType::Principal)).map(|(n,_)|n).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let original=cases.clone();
    let mut seen=BTreeSet::new();let mut groups=Vec::new();
    for base in cases{let key=serde_json::to_string(&identities.iter().map(|n|(*n,base.get(*n))).collect::<Vec<_>>()).unwrap();if !seen.insert(key){continue;}
        let mut group=vec![base.clone()];
        for (name,kind) in &p.args{let values=match kind{ir::ArgType::Bool=>vec![Value::Bool(false),Value::Bool(true)],ir::ArgType::Enum{variants}=>variants.iter().cloned().map(Value::String).collect(),_=>Vec::new()};
            for value in values{let mut candidate=base.clone();candidate.insert(name.clone(),value);if !group.contains(&candidate){group.push(candidate);}}
        }groups.push(group);
    }
    let mut out=Vec::new();for n in 0..groups.iter().map(Vec::len).max().unwrap_or(0){for group in &groups{if let Some(args)=group.get(n){out.push(args.clone());}}}
    if original.iter().any(|case|!out.contains(case)){campaign.unsupported.insert(format!("{}: directed argument grouping omits correlated/non-status alternatives; remaining combinations uncovered",p.name));}
    out
}
pub(super) fn trace_key(steps:&[Step])->Vec<(&str,&str)>{steps.iter().map(|s|(s.operation.as_str(),s.viewer.as_str())).collect()}
pub(super) fn violation(out:&mut Campaign,inv:&Invariant,steps:&[Step],viewer:&str,row:&Value,old:Option<&Value>,initial:&BTreeMap<String,Vec<Value>>,effect:&str)->bool{
    if out.violations.iter().any(|v|v.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&v.viewer==viewer&&v.reachability==Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace&&trace_key(&v.history)==trace_key(steps)){return false;}
    out.violations.push(Counterexample{reachability:Reachability::AuthoredFixtureTrace,observations:None,history:steps.to_vec(),invariant_site:inv.site.to_string(),args:BTreeMap::new(),viewer:viewer.into(),operation:steps.last().map(|s|s.operation.clone()).unwrap_or("$fixture.read".into()),effect:effect.into(),row:row.clone(),old:old.cloned(),supporting_rows:initial.clone(),rule_path:"actual authored operation/read from the trusted fixture".into(),scope:"reachable from recorded trusted fixture; finite trace, not an all-history proof".into()});true
}
pub(super) fn typed_disclosure(ty:&crate::types::Ty,data:&Value,table:&str,key:&Value,column:Option<&str>,physical:&Value)->bool{
    typed_disclosure_image(ty,data,table,key,column,physical,None)
}
pub(super) fn typed_disclosure_image(ty:&crate::types::Ty,data:&Value,table:&str,key:&Value,column:Option<&str>,physical:&Value,policy:Option<&RuleExpr>)->bool{
    use crate::types::Ty;
    let payload=|data:&Value|matches!(data,Value::Row(_))&&data.field("id").ok()==Some(key)&&column.is_none_or(|c|data.field(c).ok().is_some_and(|v|physical.field(c).ok()==Some(v)))&&policy.is_none_or(|p|!p.any_term(&|term|{

        column.filter(|c|*c!="$security:server-clock").is_some_and(|c|data.field(c).is_err()||data.field(c).ok()!=physical.field(c).ok())
    }));
    match ty{
        Ty::Row(t)|Ty::WriteRow(t) if t==table=>payload(data),
